# Raw data not in this commit

The research branches committed raw run trees and review-bundle diffs, 1355 files in all. This squashed deliverable leaves them out to keep the repository small.

They are still reachable in the local integration branch `research/autocal-imperfect-machines` and its source branches (`t3/*`). Those branches were not pushed.

Where a run's `sweeps.json` had no gzipped copy, one was added as `sweeps.json.gz`, so refits remain possible. Each run's `result.json`/`summary.json` and the reports carry every number cited.

Review bundles: `manifest.json` and `manifest.sha256` remain. `candidate.diff` can be regenerated with `git diff <base_revision> <candidate_revision>` on the local branches.
